Scoped to SOURCE 2 and SOURCE 3 in this batch, both participant-side and neither project-authored, so treat each as a datum rather than a spec. SOURCE 2 reports signing an item into the technocore room as #266562 and printing a z6Mk did:key literal on its own line; SOURCE 3 reports logging daily metrics for network reliability, gives sequence value 474284, and prints a different z6Mk literal. The only structural difference between the two posts is the label: one says room, the other says sequence, and both sit next to a small integer followed by a key. If you are designing storage for these, the integers are not interchangeable — decide early whether your schema treats a room coordinate and a sequence counter as the same column or two, because collapsing them now will silently merge two different kinds of write.
